## 549. Heb. 12:22-24(f) 549.Heb. 12'22-24 edited

Jonathan Edwards

Sermon 7 VI Vol. S N Heb. 12. 22,23,24. So -------------- and to Jesus the mediatour of the new covenant. VII. Particular. 1740 In how by this point I now 1.How a Cov. between G. & fallen Creatures 2. What Cov. that is then X is Spoken of in the text 3. Why it is called the new Cov. 4. How Jesus X is the mediator of that new Cov. 5. How Xtians are come to the mediatour. & here this I. Thing is that a Cov. between G. and fallen Creatures required a mediatour. It was not meet that G. should have to do with fallen man ..... in way of Cov. unless it was by a mediator. a Cov. is a kindly invention or a least it is ever so in G. towards his Creatures. it is a manifestation of Goodness and wonderfull Condescension in G. towards Creatures that he that is infinitely above em should should deal with them in such a way & become obliged to Creatures .... as one in Cov. with them who might deal with them only in a way of over sovreignty. But it was not meet that an offended G. should this deal with a Rebel in such a way of Condescension & Lordliness unless it was by a mediatour lest he should injure the [^016-note1] Honour of his majesty & Justice

_____________________

entering into Cov. is a always either the fruit of Peace between the parties covananting or agreeing or it is in order to Peace. but there can be no Peace between G. & Rebels but by a mediatour. G. & fallen men are enemies G. as mans Lawgiver & Judge most necessarily appear as the Enemy of him who is under the Guilt of sin & the sinners unless received by a mediator will Remain an Enemy of G. The II. Thing is to what cov. that is that is Spoken of in the Text. Answ. Tis that Cov. of Grace that X promulgated when he came unto the Θ. Tis the cov. that G. has made with the ... Chh. Vouched to the Xtian Chh as the Cov that G made with his People at Mt. Sion. wherin Pardon and Salvation are offered to Sinners & promised to believers. The parties covenanting are G. & believers. The Benefits promised in this Covenant are How the par that Eternal damnation that is Sustained by the Law against sin & a peace with G. a reconciliation after G. has been [^016-note2] provoked & displeased & his Et. wrath deserted? a deliv. from the Guilt of Sin that G. should no more Look on those that are interested in the Cov. as Sinners having no Sin imputed to them and that they should be delivered from Et. damnation that is threatened by the Law to all Sinners & from all other fruits of Gods wrath and curse & that they shewd have a real interest in the Righ. of X and acepted therin as perfectly Righ. in the Eyes of G. the Judge of all & on the account of that Righ. be received as the objects of the favor of G. & unto the state of the Children of G. ~~~ & that their natures should be more & more Sanctified that they should be delivered more & more from sin & that they should have the Image perfected in them & that the word enable them to live holy lives & bring forth Good fruit & perservere in Holiness to the End ~~~ & that he would in a way of diligent & faithful serving him give them the comforts of his Sp. peace of Conscience & Joy in the Holy Gh. Special manifes- [^016-note3] tation of his Glory & favour & a Joyfull hope of Eternal Life. ~~~ & that he would defend em from their Enemies that he would be on their side as preserve em from Satan & give em finally the victory over all Enemies. ~~~ & that at death their souls shall be perfectly freed from Sin shall ascend to be with X in Paradise in a State of Glory their bodies being still united to X resting in their Savior till the Resurrection & that then they shall Rise the souls being united to the body again which shall be made like to X’ Glorious body & that they in this risen State shall be openly acquitted before the Θ of angels & men hearing that blessed sentence come ----------------- & that then they shall with soul and body enter into the Kingd. that G. has prepared ---- ------------- & shall Enjoy Et. Life in a State of Perfect Glory & blessedness forever & ever. These are the Benefits promised in the Covenant & the Jesus. whereby the believer does with all [^016-note4] His heart close with X as the Savior renouncing all other saviors & lovers & giving up thems. to him to be united to him & to be his in all his offices. This Cov. is that which in SS is said to a sure Cov. or a Cov. well ordered in all things & sure & an Everlasting Cov. The III. Thing is to why this Cov. is called a new Cov. To such an Enquiry

-----------

I consider - This is called a new Cov. with Respect that old Cov. that was given forth from Mt Sinai. Now is a Relative from and his relation to something that in comparison of it is old when the SS. Speak of a new Cov. there is plainly supposed to have been some other Cov. in the ____ of which this succeeds . and still there is a Reference in this to something that was of old at Mt Sinai for in all the particulers mentioned in the three verses of the text the comparison is carried on between the two mountains of Mt. Sinai & Mt Sion. at Mt Sion the new cov. is given forth at Mt Sinai & the as the old cov. was given forth from Mt. Sinai. and that are two things that were [^016-note5] Exhibited at Mt Sinai with Reference to which the Cov. of Grace as Revealed by X under the in the days of the Gosp. is called a new cov.

-----------------

1. The Cov. of works that G. had formerly entered into with men in the state in which he was first created was given forth at Mt. Sinai. The Cov. of works was that given forth to the Chil. of Israel. G. did not properly then make a Cov. of w. with the Chil. of Isr. because they were already fallen he did not enter into a Cov. of W. with them for they were not in & Capacity for it they were already fallen Creatures & so not capable of Et. Life by such a Cov. But yet G. did truly give forthe the Cov. of w. from Mt Sinai he did at that time renewedly reveal and propose that Cov. of works to the Chil. of Israel. From that mountain for their Conviction to prove them & make them sensible of their own miserable & undone Condition & make em sensible of their necessity of a mediatour. So the Cov. of works was given out to em in all the strictness & severity to be as a Schoolmaster & [^016-note6] Takes the Chh from its minority & fits it for the Coming of X. The Law Shut em up to the faith that should afterward be Rev. revealed

-------------

Tis Certain that the Cov. of works was then given forth to the Chil. of Israel because the SS Speaking of the ............ Cov. of Grace & comparing the Law & Gospel. & the works of the Law with the faith of Jesus X often Speaks of the Law as being given of old by moses. now the Law thus Spoken of is the very same thing as the Legal Cov. & which is the same thing the Cov. of w. The Law was given at Mt Sinai with all its strictness & terrors , with its thunders & lightnings & curses saying cursed is every man that continues not. Deut.27.26 Gal.3.10

____________

Now that is nothing else but giving forth the Cov. of w. with its strict exaction of Perfect Obed. on pain of Et. damnation. 2. The Cov. of Grace is delivered given forth by X [^016-note7] is called a new Cov. with the Respect to the same Cov. of Grace as Given forth in its old Legal dispensation by Moses. Tho the Cov. of works was given forth at Mt. Sinai yet G. did not only that was not the only Cov. that was given revealed there to the Chil. of Israel. if it had it would have tended to drive them into Despair. but the Cov. But there was a Cov. that G. did not only reveal but which he properly entered into with that people at that mountain whereby they became his Covenant People and were as it were Espoused to hims.. and this could be no other than the Cov. of Grace. G. did really reveal the Cov. of Grace at Mt Sinai tho it was more obscurely by far than X afterw. Revealed it & tho it was delivered in more Legal manner. G. did really renew with the Chil. of Is. in the wild. That Cov. that he had made with Abraham 430 years before. which the SS expressly teaches to be the Cov. of Grace & therf. G. when he comes to the people [^016-note8] in the wil. encourages em with that that he was the G. of Abraham. The Cov. of Grace is virtually contained in those words in the Preface to the Ten C. Which words G. Spoke at Mt Sinai I am the L. thy G. -

____________________________

it was only by the Cov. of Grace that G. was the L. their G. & the w. of R. by X was represented by Gods bringing them out of the Land of Egy. out of the H. of B. there Spoken of. & the Cov. of Grace was contained in the Ceremonial Law that was given at Mt. Sinai wherin such sacrifices were commanded & so many things representing & shadowing forth the Redemption of X.

____________________________

That old Legal dispensation of the Cov. of Grace tho it was Essentially the same Cov. yet because it differed . . . with that which X Revealed in its Evangelical D. dispensation yet because it differed circumstantially is sometimes spoken of in SS as another Cov. so it is in the 8 of Heb. beginning with the 5 v. ------- [^016-note9]

_______________________________________________

Here the old Cov. that he Speaks of is manifestly the old Legal dispens. of works & not the Cov. of works because He speaks of that Cov. that the antient priests were ministers of of & speaks of X as a mediator of a better Cov. than those priests were as you may see.

_______________

& it appears more fully still in the beginning of the following chap. the 9 chap. . v. 1 --------

____________________________

The Cov. that had these endowments was not the Cov. of works ----------

_______________

So that by new & old Cov. in SS. Sometimes is meant the same as new & old Testament & therf. the Phrases seemed to be used indifferently to signify the same thing. When it is said in the text that X was the mediator of the new Cov. in the margin it is ------

--------------

& we find the phrases used in the same sense in the 7 of Heb. 22.v. tis said J. was the surety of a better Test. - in the next chap 8 tis said he is the mediator of a better Cov. both meaning the same thing. The Cov. of Grace as promised by [^016-note10] X in its Evangelical dispensa. seems to be called a N.Cov. with Respect to those others Constitutions on two accounts. Because it succeeds them in order of time & is Established in their stead. First the Cov. of works is Revealed & then the Cov. of Grace & first the Legal dispensation of the Cov. of Grace is established & then the Evangelical. the Later dispensation takes place of the former. the former waxing old & vanishing away the Latter is Established in its Stead. Heb. 8. 13. in that he saith a new Cov. he hath made the first old now that which decayeth & becometh old is ready to vanish away. 2. It seems also to be called a new Cov. on account of its Superiour Excellency because artificial things are commonly in their best state while new & because old things are removed to make way for new ones for the sake of their Superiour Excellency. therf. we find the word new sometimes denotes the Great Excellency of the thing that is said to be so. Thus X promises to him that overcomes that he will give him a new name Rev.2.7. Which imports that X will give him an excellent & put upon him Excellent Honour & dignity. So the Chh is said to sing a new song Rev. 5.9. i.e. - [^016-note11]

_________________

and from time to time those things Spiritual things that are Spoken as new and more Excellent than the old ---- Heaven & Earth ---------- old shall not be Remembered ------ new Jerusalem ---------

___________

so the new Cov is a more Excellent Cov. than the old Cov. of Grace - more Excel. - than Cov. of w. - shewing more of the Glory of G. -------- better promises --------- a surer Cov. ----an Everlasting Cov. ---- the first sure on Gods side but uncertain on mens but ----

__________________

so the Evangelical dispensation is more Excellent- ----------- Light clearer Grace of G. made manif. & more abundant ------- The verse explained promises & those that are most insisted are more excellent. --------

___________________

The Chh with whom the Cov. in its Evangelical dispensation is made is a more Sp. Heavenly & Excellent Constitution. I come now to the IV IV. Thing Proposed which is to shew how that J.X. [^016-note12] is the mediatour of this new Cov. as to this Cov. of w. that had no mediatour.

____

The Cov. of Grace in its old dispensa. which is sometimes called the old Cov. had a typical mediatour. Insofar -

_____

........ Priests How Priests especially as such entered into the Holy of Holies.

_______

as has been observed before the Honnour of God’s maj. & Justice will not suffer him to treat with a Rebel by way of Cov. but by a mediatour & X is the mediatour of the new Cov. in the following Respects. 1. He is the Person who brings the Cov. from G. to us Moses of old brought ---- Returned this words to the People. Speak thou with us say they. Exod. 20.19

________________

brought the Tablets of the Cov.

______

wrote the Book of the Cov.

______

and as the Priests instructed the People

_______

so brought down from Heaven

______

reveals it makes known the mind & will of G. concerning mens [^016-note13] Salva. Herein mostly consists the prophetical office. When he came he brought it presently

-------

He sent his Sp. more fully to Reveal it by his apostles ..... agreeable to his promise

_____

to them he shall take of mine & shall shew it unto you.

________________

He brings the Cov. & made it by his Sp. an Effectual calling. The Promises of the Cov. are first made work X to X as the Head & Representative of all the elect & through him are made to believers. Tit.1.2. in hope of Et. Life which G. that cannot lie 2 Tim.1.9. who hath saved us & called us as of old promises made to Abraham as the F. of. 3. He is the mediatour that has procured all [^016-note14] the blessings promised in the Cov. 4. He is the Person that has been Confirmed the blessings of the Cov. sealed with his blood 5. the Blessings of the Cov. are not obtained in any other way but in union with him. 6. The consent to the Cov. on our [^016-note15] part is brought to the F. through him as moses our Faith & prayers are of avail with G. only through hims. as head

----------

7. He first Receives the blessing of the Cov. of Grace of the F. in hims. as Head.

________

8. He is the Person through which the blessings of the Cov. are actually bestowed. [The following section is within an oval.] 1. Use may be to convince X sinners of their misery in this they have no mediatour to shew between G. & thems. 1. Consid. you are a stranger to the Cov. of Promise 2. The wrath of G. abiding upon 3. How dreadful it will be [to] die & Go & appear before G. without a mediatour

---------------

2. Use of Exh. to come to their mediatour and trust in him. [This is in a second oval below the first.] 1. consider how fit a person X is for that office. 2. what he will do for you in it. [Closed the second oval.] V. Enq. is How Xtians are come to their me- [^016-note16] diator

___________

Answ. This Implies three things.

_____

1. They accept of him. accepting of X by faith is very often called coming to him in SS . X Invites heavy Laden sinners to come to him that he may give em Rest & promises that he that comes to him he will in no wise cast out.

______________

2. They are united. union with X is the first & most Immediate Consequence of acceptance of him. Xtians have a vital union with X. they are come to him & are ingrafted onto him & become branches on him members of his body. They are come to him so that he is come to dwell with them & in them by his Holy Spr. - ~~~and there is also a Relative union.. They are come to be nearly related to X. Their souls are brought to X as the Kings daughter is led in unto a Glo. Prince to be united to him in Sp. wedlock. ~~~There is a Covenant union between & X & the soul of a Xtian they are united by the mutual bed of a Cov. whereby he is theirs and they his there is such an union that they have a mutual propriety in Each other.

_______________

There is a Legal union between them so that they are one in Law and Looked upon & accepted as one in what concerns the Law by G. the Judge of all. 3. They are come to as they communion with him in his Benefits. is the Immediate Consequence of union or a partaking of the Benefits of X’ mediation. Such as freedom from Guilt prove unto G. a Joint title to the Et. Inhe participation of Life & comfort. & a Joint title to the Et. Inheritance & Kingd.

---------------

& among other benefits they are brought to of X’ mediation they have a title to dwell with X forever to be where he is & to behold his Glory. Joh.17.24. F. I will that those whom thou hast given should C End of sermon seven. [John 17.24 . passage reads: I desire that they also whom thou hast given me be with me where I am, that they may behold my glory, which thou hast given me.]

Chp. of Levit. the literal mt sion was the place when the old Covenant was made or the Cov. of Grace [-] in its old dispen sation was made & Confirmed with the Chil. of Israel moses who was the typical mediatour of that old Cov. took the Book of the Cov. and read in the Audience of the People [^017-note1] & when they had said all that the L. hath said will we do and be obedient then moses took the Blood of the sacrifice and sprinkled it on the People and said behold the Blood of the Cov. which the L. hath made with you concerning all these words . That was the blood of sprinkling by which the f old Cov. or first Testament was Confirmed H In the Text the Aposlte speaks of the blood of sprinkling by which the new Test. Cov. is Confirmed. How Xtians are come to this blood of sprinkling The I Enq. is what blood blood this is that [^017-note2] is here spoken of Ans. the blood of X the blood of sprinkling of that was by which the blood Cov. of th blood of Goats & Calves with water & scarlet wool & Hyssop but the blood by which the new Cov. is Confirmed is th X' own blood as Heb 9. 23.

in the literal mt sion the blood of bulls & Goats was the blood of sprinkling by which the un clean was sanctified to the purifying of the flesh but X' own blood is the blood of sprinkling that to purify the unclean soul Heb 9. 11 & c--------

The II Enq. is why X' this blood of X is called the blood of sprinkling

__________________

Ans. 1. because it availed avails nothing but by becoming applied to the soul. the bl However sufficient this blood w is in it self yet it Can be of no service to u or benefit to us un less tis applied to us our souls cant be Cleansed by it if no application be made of it This application is by means of two things partly by a work of the sp. working [^017-note3] faith in that blood m awakening the soul need of the blood of X & making it sensible of the sufficiency of that blood & bringing it to that fountain that is set open for Judah & Jerusalem for sin & for uncleanness & Partly an act of Grace Consequent of that [-] work of the sp. Justifying the sinner by this the Guilt of sin is actually purged away & the sinner through the blood of X stands Perfectly Clean [-] or pure in the sight of G Thus the blood the blood of X is applied to the soul & without such application it [-] Can have no effect for its Cleansing

2 This application of the blood of X was of old typified represented by the sprinkling of the blood of the sacrifices on the --------- so the blood of the sacrifices was to be sprinkled & so the water of Purifying that represented the blood of X was also to be sprinkled upon the Persons that were to be Cleansed by it Numb. 8. 7. so that the water of separation was to be sprinkled upon him that touched the dead body of a man as in the 19. of Numb. & & hence tis prophecied of X in Isai 52. 15. That [^017-note4] th he should spinkle many nations . The

III Enq. is what things those were that were spoken by the blood abel Abel. that there is --- are Referred to in the words

Ans. The blood of Abel [-] cried for vengeance. The Bloo Abel was if he was not the first Good man that died yet he was in all probability the first Good man that was put to death for his Righteousness . for [--] Abel was so and was slain for his Righ 1 Joh. 3. 12 ---------------------------------------

-----------

He was one of X' he was a member of the mystical body or of the Chh of X and seems in his day to have been the Chief of the Holy seed that G. had in the Θ and therefore the Cruelty that was done to him was in some sense done to X as X says in that ye have done it to one of the Least of these my Brethren ye have done it unto me but He was as it may be Looked upon as the first that ever died a martyr that was persecuted to death for his piety . His blood cried from the Ground for divine [-] divine vengeance to G. as G. says to Cain Gen 4. 10. The voice of thy Brothers blood crieth to me from the Ground. Thus the blood [^017-note5] [--] Abel spoke after he was de had a voice after he was dead it spoke it spoke after he was dead w which is what the Apostle has Reference to in Heb. 11. 4. so sacrif. than Cain by which he obtained witness that he was righ. G. testifying of his Gifts & by it he being dead yet speaketh Heis blood spoke as it cried to G. & what it Cried to him for was Revenge for that G. his wrath & Curse on him that had made hims. Guilty of his blood.

The blood of these two Persons might well be Compared thus together gecause they were both Righ. tho one but imperfectly & the other Perfectly so the [--] Abel w the one by a derivative dependent Righ. the other had Righ. in hims. & they both were hated & made by the malignity of the sin & Corruption of mankind it was the sin of man that Killed abel & so it was the sin of man that X Killed X the nature & tendency of sin is to attempt to kill G. as appeared b by what was done came to pass in fact when G. became man & was Capa bale of being Killed & all the same sinnners that are saved by the blood of have in Effect been guilty of Killing of X for that their sin nature & that the sins that they Committed had that tendenc showed if was it had been in their power they would do it & the Con sequence of their sin has actually been the death of X their sins a were Costly to X they Cost him his blood But yet his blood don't Cry for vengeance to those sinners that believe come to him as Abels did

But 1. what X' blood cries for Peace & Pardon for the Guilty for those that have been his murth murtherous Enemies when they came to him this is the voice of his blood to G. Let these po poor sinfull wretched who have have hated me & have been my malicious murtherous Enemies Let them be forgiven Let all their Iniquities be blotted out & Let em be accepted as the objects that they deserve but Let em have peace with thee Let em be have not have souls [--] terrour of Conscience any as Cain [^017-note6] had but Let em be delivered from the sp. of bondage & Let 'em have peace of Conscience the peace of G. that passes all understanding

his blood cries to G. that he may be entitled [^017-note7] to a Glo. Inheritance & that not that they might be slain to in Revenge as Abels blood Cried for the death of Cain but that they may have Et. Life. There are Three things in the blood of X by which it speaks such things . viz its sufficiency to merit them & [--] its appointmt of G. the F for that End & X design in offering it up to G. & its sufficiency to merit them

_______________________________________________

1 X was appointed of G. to be an high Priest to offer up his blood to these Ends that he might secure such things as these for sinners

_______________________________________________

2. It was not only the Fathers will It was X' design in offering shedding his blood these thereby to Obtain these Good things for sinners that should come to him. It This was not only the will of the F but it was X' will. It was a thing agreed upon between the F and the son that his blood should be spilt to this End . & when G. the F sent him into the Θ on this Errand he most willingly Came he said Lo I come I delight ------------ To save [-] poor sinners his murtherous Enemies [^017-note8] was the Great [--] thing that moved him to yield hims. up to be slain. he Loved us & Gave hims. for us he had a Great & Earnest desire that sin if we may that sinners might obtain these Good things & th to that End he spilt his blood his Laying down his Life was but an xexpression of his this desire for this he Earnestly prayed wh a little before his blood was shed for this he offered up strong Crying & tears in that bloody sweat so that he was the subject of in the Garden it was for these Good things that he [-] wrestled with G. so Earnestly in the days of his flesh when he was in such an Agony & his sweat was as it were Great drops of Blood falling down to the Ground

## 552. Rom. 11:7(b) 552. Rom 11'7(b) edited

Jonathan Edwards

______________________________________________

Rom 11. 7. But the election hath obtained and the rest were blinded. I have already in speaking to the former part of the verse what that is that is spoken of as not being obtained by some viz th Acceptance with G. & Et. here spoken of viz the People Israel that Peop had alone been favoured with means of Grace for a Great may ages and were a People that were in the days in the days of X & his Apostles were the first that were favoured with the offer of sal vation [-] the means of Grace & were strove with for some times because the Apostles turned to the Gentiles X said he was not sent but to the Lost sheep of the H. of Is. and the Apost math 15. 24. and the Apostles at first spoke to preached to the Jews only. Acts 11. 19.

_____________________

& even after the Calling of the Gentiles was begun yet the Apostles wherever they came if there were any Jews there they made the first offer to the Jews in every city they in the first place went into the synagogues of the Jews before they preached to the Heathen as we read from time to tim time of the apos tle Paul. in the acts [^019-note1]

_____________

In these this Latter part of the verse which I am now to Insist upon we have an account of a very remarkeable distinction that was made among the diff this people with Respect to the success of the Gospel among them & their obtaining its bene that salvation that was offered. Tho they were all the Posterity of the same ancestors Holy ancestours Abraham Is. & Jac. & tho they were all favoured alike with external Privilidges and means of Grace and also all hop words we may observe

___________________________________________

1. The different Persons spoken of The Election not Elected there is a Great difference but originally there is no Inherent diff . They are in thems. alike they have the same nature . so [-] alike in their Pedigree they descended of the same Ancestours and were of the same nation & their advantages were the same they all had the same Gospel Preached to em & Enjoyed the is only in Gods is only in the distinctions that sovereign Grace makes among them some are Elected and others are Left some are [---] those that G. of his sovereign Good Pleasure has been pleased to Look upon [--] set his Love upon from Et & ordain emm to Et. Life others he has Left to perish

_________________________________________________

2. we may observe their different success in what they sought for. The Election obtained those among that People that from Et. had been Chosen of G. to Et. Life they obtained their Justification & salvation . But the Rest were blinded . blind men wander in the dark & cant find what they seek for but weary thems. as the men of sodom did when they were struck with blindness to find the door they never find it & so never Enter in

______________________

all the Rest were blinded but only those that were of the Election of Grace Tho there were many thousands of the Jews that in the Apostles times were Enlightened & sa brought home to X yet they were but few in Comparison of the whole nation agreeable to the Prophecy [-] of Isai which is cited by the Apostle 9 Chp. 27. Tho the numb of the Chil. ----------------

--------------------------

all the Rest but only that Remnant were blinded and their city was a little while suffered a ter rible national destruction . & their Posterity are this very day.

_________________________________________________

Doc. Tho a People that live under means are wont in General to seek & hope for salvation yet tis the Election only that obtain & the Rest are blinded.

_________________________________________________

Here are Thfour Propositions 1. a people that live under means of Grace are wont in General to seek & hope for salva.

______________________

2. That tho it be so yet tis Those among a People [-] that are under means of Grace that are of Gods Election do & w will surely obtain salvation.

________________

3.[-] The Rest of such a People will not obtain

__________________
