1. It will Render all that will be terrible and amazing to other on that day to be pleasant & Joyfull unto you. There will be many things on that day that will be Exceeding Dreadfull to the bigger part of men that last day that Great day of the L will be the most dreadfull day to them that Ever was seen. the day of death is a terrible day to men many are all their lives time subject to Bondage through fear of death but the day of Judgmt is will be a more terrible day for wicked men than that That is so dreadfull a day that the very devils [^022-note8] those stoud proud haughty & sturdy sp. tremble at the thoughts of that day . but if you Choose this good part it will thoroughly secure you from all the terrours of that day. there will be nothing terrible to you in [it] yea nothing but what will be Joyf. Christs first appearance in the Clouds of Heavein coming to Judgmt will be an amazing sight to the wicked when X first makes his appearance in Heaven it will be very sudden an unexpected sight to the and as they were in sodom in the days of Lot in no Expecta. of any such thing nor any prepa their sins full of in the midst of their carnal pursuits & wallowing in wickedness then shall they suddenly be surprized with this Great sight of a wonderf. Glo Person & divine Person in the Hea making his appearance in the Heavens at a Great in the majesty of G. & with an ten thousand thous thous. times ten thous. Glo sight as they never be saw before and how will [^022-note9] they be terrified affrightened. who can express the sudden terrour & amazemt that will [--] be struck into the Hearts of all the wicked of the Θ at that sight. but if you have an Interest in the this best Good that has been spoken of it will not be at all terrifying to you but the pleasantest & Joyfullest sight that ever your Eyes beheld

many at that time will be surprized in the night for to some parts of the face of the Earth it will be in the dead of the night when X shall first the first notice shall be given of X' coming . & then there will be midnight cry indeed many will be suddenly waked out of sleep with the news . some probably will be waked with the universal light of X' Glory shining down on the Earth as he Comes appears in Heaven or with the universal & awfull Thunders Thunders & Lightnings which probably will attend his coming

but it will not be terrible to the Godly they shall recieve the news with Joy as the most Joyf news that t Ever they heard . & when they come [--] to behold that sight which is so terrible so amazes others it will appear Glorious & Joyfull to them & a sweet & Glo. sight to them The Θ then will be full of shrieking & yelling of young & old Great & small Every where & full of trembling & astonishmt but then those that have this best good will be full of Joy none of the thunders & Lightnings of that day will be terrible to them tho doubtless they will be such as will show the awfull majesty of & mighty Power of G. a thous. much more than any that ever have been before. but all will be pleasant to those that have this Good part. & when the Great trumpet shall be blown that Last trump of G. shall be sounded by the archangel one of the migh tiest of all the Angels & the sound shall shall [^022-note11] shake ear heaven & earth & strike such a hor tude it shall be a pleasant sweet sound to those hav that have Chosen the Good part

## 556. Rom. 11:10 556. Rom 11'10 edited

Jonathan Edwards

_____________________________________________

Rom 11. 10. --------- and bow down their back always In this Context is set forth the Great difference that was made between some & others of the Chh of I Nation of the Jews in that age by the sovereign of pleasure [--] in Election The Apostle observes that the Election had obtained mercy v. 7. & then it described the Exceeding miserable state of the Rest of them those that were not Elected & the awfull Curse that they are under. our text is part of described part of that Curse that they is upon such The Curse that is here that those among the Jews that were not Elected were laid under we have in three verses 8. 9. & 10. according as it is wr

-----------------------------------------------

-----------------------------------------------

This description is taken out of the old T. as is intimated by the words with which it is in troduced in the 8 v. according as it is written The Curse here described Consists of two parts

_____

1 a being given over of God to blindness. & 2. to an E Earthly & Carnal mindedness.

______________

The former part of their curse viz. Their being given over to blindness & stupidity is described in the 10. v. & 8. v. & in the former part of the 10. v. 8. according as it is written G. hath given them a sp. of slumber Eyes that they should not see & Ears that they should not hear unto this day . which words are cited from the of Isaiah. again in the 10. verse The Latter part of their Curse is d viz their being given over to a sensual Earthly mind is described in the 9. v. Let their Table ------------------------------ ----------------------- The words are cited from 69. Ps. 22. v Their table becomes a snare & a trap i.e their Earthly Enjoymts become sn a snare to them by drawing awa possessing their Hearts & by th Enslaving their affections Thus these two parts of their Curse are described in the two foregoing verses [--] from different passages of the Old Testamt. but then the v. of the Text They are both described from one & the same passage of the O. T. Les their Eyes be darkened that they may not see & bow down their back alway . in the former part of the v. Let their Eyes be darkened is described the former part of the Curse viz their being given up to blindness & stupidity [^023-note1] & in the Latter part bow down their back alway is described the Latter part of their curse viz their being given over to ssensuality a carnal & Earthly mind Their backs is bow are bowed down alwaies & their faces are forever set towards the Earth to signifie that their hearts are forever set upon mens sensual from the 69. Ps. 23. v. the words there are Let their Loins be darkened that they may not see not & make their Loins Continually to shake here in stead of bowing down their back alway as it is the words are in the text it is make their Loins continually to shake but tho the words are different yet the sense is the same for the Continually shaking of their loins denotes the weakness of them They shake as being unable to support the body in an Erect posture & totter under the weight of the body as being unable to support it in an Ere it . The words in the text describe the weakness of their loins as backs unable to support the body in an Erect posture they are bowed down to the Earth & have no strength to Lift up thems. [^023-note2] their spiritual disease of soul that is here spoken of was Represented by that disease of body that X cured in the woman that we read of in Luke 13. 11. 12. and behold there was a woman which had a sp. of Infirmity Eighteen years and was bowed together & could in no wise lift up her self. she had a sp. of In firmity or weakness such a weakn. in her Loins that she Could n by no means support her body in an Erect posture & there in the following verses we have an account how X cured her & Loosed her from her Infirmity & Laid his hands on her & enabled her to Lift up her self from her the Earth. As I have often observed to you the diseases & Calamities that X by his miracles healed per sons of were all of them rep figures & Representations of those sp. diseases that he came into the Θ to heal mens souls of . & so doubt less this disease of the woman was a Repre sentation of that sp. Calamity spoken of in the Text whereby when it is said that wards to towards the Earth so that they were not able [^023-note3] to Lift up thems. & that tis their hearts being bowed down & bent on the things of the Earth that is meant is Confirmed by the foregoing v. where it is said that their Table is become a snare & a trap to em & as this a [----] These are the words preceeding the text what is said in the text & also in the Psalm whence the words are cited . for which way do m do mens sensual & Earthly Enjoymts become a snare to them but by their setting their hearts upon them

_________________________________________________

doc. some men are Laid under such a Curse that they are forever bowed down to the Earth without being able ever to Lift up & never are able to Lift up themselves.

________________________________________________

In speaking to this subject I would show in the first place 1 place. in what sense they may be said to be bowed down to the Earth 2. that some are thus bowed down forever without Ever being able to Lift up thems. & 3 how this is by by Gods Curse upon them

_______________________________________________

I. I would show in what sense they me men are may be said to be bowed down to the Earth. They are so in two Respects viz. in their carnal affections & their carnal [^023-note4] pursuits

_______________________________________________

1. They are so in their Carnal & Earthly affections They see no better natural men see no better good yea they see no other good than Earthly good & theref. their affetions go wholly out after these things their hearts are Knit to the things of of They dont set their affections on things above but on things on the Earth. their hearts are Knit to things them. They make Choice of them as their highest Good & place their happiness & hence they so they Idolize them & set em up in Gods stead. this Θ is truly every natural mans God . he makes Choice affections as with invisible strong bonds their minds are full of h Earthly thoughts & meditations there is little if we could look into their hearts & see what passes there we should see in their thoughts & meditations we should see little Else besides carnal & Earthly objects by day or by night. They are slaves to Eager desires after these things & their minds are full of Expectati carried away with carnal hopes & Expecta. tions. mens having thei [^023-note5]

_____________________________________________

2. The same mens having their hearts thus set on the things E carnal objects may be fitly represented as having their faces set on them Creatures they are have their Chief good in those things that are of a sensual & Earthly nature & indeed those things were made to be their high est Good & theref they are not made in an Erect Posture or to go upon upright but with their heads down towards the Ground.

_____________________

natural men may sometimes think much about things of a Religious & spiritual n other things than the things of the Θ through a force that their minds are Laid under by fear & the Terrours of the Law th set before them but every natural mans heart is set on Earthly objects . some more Especially on those of one Kinds others Chiefly those of another Kind. some have their affections mainly [--] bent on Θly profits or riches . for others on Earthly pleasures many men are bowed down to the Earth in sensuality they have their hearts bowed down after impure delights & pleasure as [-] swine have go with their heads bowed down after seeking after filthiness [^023-note6] some have their thoughts & all the powers of their souls Captivated by some fleshly Lust

_______

& some set their hearts mainly on Θly Θly honour which is but an Earthly object & tho in one sense [-] they that set their hearts upon it may be said to Look high yet Considering the meanness & of that hon & worthlessness of that honour which they seek it may truly be Represented as going with their backs bowed down to the Earth.

______________________________________________

2. men They may be said to be bowed down to the Earth by reason of their being devoted to carnal & Earthly pursuits that which their affections are set upon that their they seek after they go with their backs bowed down to the Earth [--] seeking Eagerly after those things that are Earthly they make it their business to some seek somethings to Gratify their Carnal desires as swine make it their business to root in the Earth after their food [-] pursuing after the Earthly Enjoymts is their continual slavery and as therefore may be represented as those that alwaies have their backs bowed down as men are wont to have [^023-note7] while working[?] & toiling in the Earth & as slaves that are Ruled over by cruel task masters & are as it were broken with hard bondage are bowed together with the Burthen of their continual drudgery

_______________

carnal men by Reason of be their being devoted in their affections & pursuits to E carnal objects & Enjoymts may be said to be bowed down Chiefly on two accounts

____________________________________________________

1. because of the Inferiour nature of these objects they are mean & Low things and may fitly be cmpared to the dust of the Earth on that account as they are called . amox 2. 7. They are things that it is below the dignity of mans nature for him to set his heart upon & theref when men they are below vastly inferious to those Enjoymts that the human nature is made capable of & was designed for they are below mans proper End & therefore when men devo [-] devote thems. to them without stooping or bringing thems. down below thei as it were below their proper stature below that dignity & honour which properly belongs to mans nature by the capacity & End which the Creatour has given it nebachadness nebachednezzer was debased be- [^023-note8] low that dignity of of a man when he was drive from men & instead of that Erect posture that he men belongs to men he had his back bowed down & went on all fours like beas Earth did eat Grass as oxen. so which is a lively image of that debasement of nature that men are the subjects of that set their are devoted to carnal Enjoymts they are as it were bowed down below the proper Erect posture of the human nature to the Posture of beasts

___________________________________________________

2. because the things that they are devoted to are of the Earth. Earthly profits & Earthly pleasures & Earthly honour things that have their Existence and are all of Earthly original & things that have their Existence no where but in the present Earthly state. so that the Earth is that which they set their hearts upon & pursue after & in order to which they must bowed bow down their backs. if what for what they seek is that which is made for man to tread under his feet the Earth is made for us to tread upon to set our feet on & not our Hearts he is set in an Erect [-] it is an observa. even of one of the ---- heathen that man was made in [^023-note9] Erect Posture different from all other Creatures that he might lift up his face to the heavens

______________________________________________

II. thing Proposed to be shown is that some persons are thus bowed down forever without Ever being able to lift up thems. Their hearts are alwaies bowed down they Cleave to Earthly things as tho they were fastened to em wi as strongly by their lusts as tho' they were bound down to 'em with br in crue this cruel bondage from which they can no more deliver thems. than the Israelites could deliver thems. from their Egyptian Taskmasters what They are sold under sin as in Rom. 7. 14. & nothing ever avails to deliver them but they Go thus bent down Eagerly pursuing after these mean Earthly Enjoymts sensual pleasures and & profits [-] against all warnings & Reproofs & against the the Light of their own Reason & against the checks & Rebukes of their own Consciences . They ha are told of that are far preferable to those Earthly things & are very much Exhorted to take off their [^023-note10] ----- from those things & set upon these more noble objects . but [--] they Cant be persuaded to lift up their heads & turn their Eyes heavenwards they are told how soon those Earthly things that they are hunting after will fail 'em & then they will be Left miser able bu & how miserable they will be at Last & their Reason tells em it will be so. but yet all avails nothing but still their heads are dust of the Earth it may they will some time or other Lift up thems. & seek Heavenly things but it never Comes to pass if we could view as they are from year to year still their they Remain bowed down towards the Earth . somet it m some of them are often smitten in Conscience they are afraid of the Consequence & make many thoughts to Leave their carnal the gratifying of their Lusts & the pursuit of their carnal objects but they dont never do it but still their backs Remain bowed down . They some times flatter thems. that they are delivered [^023-note11] from their bondage & slavery to C their Lusts when they have in some measure Reformed or because they are abstained for a while. but it is really no such thing when the Tempta. Returns they are as they were before there has been no thorough divorce of their hearts from the things of the Θ but they are still they are delivered they are [--] miserably decieved for & are very far from deliverance their Lusts still have em in Chains Just like the bird that the fowler Lets go & it Imagines it self to be at Liberty but the & flies with & ------ its wings with the pleasing hope th of having freedom hence Liberty henceforward . but presently it finds it self ----- by a string [----] & the fowler unexpectedly pulls it down from its flight & so time after time as they are servants to sin so they are servants to mammon. & their God is in their belly. their belly is their master tyrannizing over Tim 7 Philip. 3. 19. --------------- Their belly is their master tyrannizing over them in a cruel manner [^023-note12] the [--] of the woman X says of the woman that X healed that had which his [-] a sp. of Infirmity & was bowed together for 18 years so that she could in no wise lift up her self that satan had bowed her in that pos as v. 16. & ought not this woman whom satan hath bound so this 18 years be Loosed from this bond on the sabbath day. she was bowed down with her face towards the Earth & turned down in that posture by satan & so it is with those that we are speaking of the devil has bowed em down by the bonds of their Lusts so that they can in no wise lift up thems. & so they continue forever Enslaved to their Lusts they as long as they live if they live till they are old their backs are thus bowed down with their Lusts till they are bowed with age yea till th & till they bowed themselves on their give up the Ghost . I come now to show in the

_____________________________________________

III. Place that when it is thus with persons th it is from a Curse of G. that such Persons lie under are Laid under. This is implied in the Text for for there G. is spoken of as the doer of it. Let their Eyes be darkened that they may not [^023-note13] see & bow -- The words are directed to G. & are in the form of an Imprecation of such a curse from him on some persons & in the Ps. from whence the words are Cited they are properly a prophetical imprecation or curse on the Enemies of the Chh. for tho no may pray for Evil to his Enemies as moved by his own sp. as a sp. of hatred to them yet Prophets that were moved by the sp. of G. did often Imprecate Curses on the Enemies wicked men which thhey did not do in the so we read that Elisha turned back & Looked on the Chil. that mocked him & Cursed them in the name of the L. 2. K. 2. 24. . so that Psalmist in the words Psalm 69. Ps whence the words are quoted curses the Enemies of Gods Chh. in the name of the L. & theref the by his sp. & theref the Curse sh will come up actually comes upon many such from G.

when it is thus persons thus are alwaies bowed down towards the Earth it is from a curse of G. that they are Laid under

_______

1 as it was from the Curse of G. that man was first brought under the Power & Dominion of his Lust. for the Corruption of nature comes [^023-note14] upon man as a punishmt of the first transgression thus inas much as it was & as a Curse that G. took away his holy sp. from man [-] & Left him to hims. as a punishmt of his first transgression but the necessary Consequence of that departure of Gods sp. was mans falling into an universally corrupt state wholly under the dominion of Lust & so all natural men are bow down to the Ear bowed & bound down by their lusts to the Earth as a Curse of G. upon them who has left them to the Power of those Lusts .

____________________

2. Tis still the fruit of a Greater Curse of G. when Persons that live under means of Grace are finally & forever left under the Power of those Lusts for G. offers mercy to all that live under means of Grace . & dont finally give em over to their hearts Lusts till they have Rejected mercy & abused the means of their deliverance . when he thus finally Leaves men & gives em over fo to be forever bowed towards the Earth. tis the fruit of this terrible wrath for a Rejected & despised saviour Application Application I. use may be of self Examinat. I would take occasion from what has been said to put persons upon Examining thems. with Respect to two things

________

1 Enqu. whether or no they have ever been delivered from such bondage as this is do you not was a slave to your Lusts [-] you saw no better Good than the Enjoyments of this Θ and was bowed down in [-] to the Earth by Earth in Earthly affections & carnal affections & Earthly Pursuits & could by no means de lift up your self had [-] of but your Lusts baffled all the Reproofs & warnings you had & all the Checks of of your own Consc. & your fears of the Conseq. & all your Re solu own Resolutions . so that tho' you might make some attempts to Reform yet all [---] all proved in vain when the Temptation Returned you [--] found that your soul was not at liberty but was still held in as in the stri satans string

___________

& have you ever been delivered from being bound down to the Earth by such a sp. of Infirmity have you has Jesus X Ever made you strait enabled you to lift up your your face to self & turn your face from the Earth towards Heaven . & so that your carnal desires & affections have no longer dominion over [^023-note15] you . have you ever that persons that heretofore have been thus bowed towards the Earth [--] are [----] set at liberty & do are Enabled to walk in an Erect posture. that miracle of X' healing the woman that had been bowed down eighteen years & making her strait . & & enabling her to lift up her self & to walk Erect was an Image of the w. of Conv. as almost all X' miracles were & in that Conversion X thus delivers souls fr in the work of Conversion so he proclaims liberty to captives & the opening of the prison to them that are bound agreeable to Isai 61. 1. ind and indeed a true & thorough Conversion can scarcely be better characterized & distinguished from a seeming & false conversion than in such a deliverance as this the poor Enslaved souls be ing thus set at liberty. whatever high affections the soul may have in a false Con in the progress of things that the soul is y in its abject slavery to its Lusts & Returns as a dog to his vomit & as the sow that was washed ------------------- the soul in a counterfeit Conv. is like a bird [^023-note16] that seems to be set at liberty by the fowler but is really all the while tied by the Leg & theref flies but a little way before it finds it self to be in bondage as before. but in true Conversion the soul is as a bird that is indeed set at Liberty from the snare of the fowler in a [-] Enq. theref. whether your heart has ever been weaned from the Θ & divorced from your Lusts have you ever parted with the Θ for X & has there been such a Change made in you that however you are greatly affected by the Remains of your Lusts yet sin they have not dominion over you as they used to have have you a heart to part with all for part with all for X & so to give your self to him without Reserve for this is for a person to be Loosed from his sp. of Infirmity & to be lifted up from the Earth to Look towards Heaven . have is your Heart your affections & Choice mainly on Heavenl & heavenly things. and have you an heart to part with all for Heaven. natural men have no power to do so their carnal affections are so strong & have such Power over em that they cant part [^023-note17] with their lusts for Heaven th sin is s theref tis Impossible that they should be willing to part with it for any thing Else & then & then only is a man delivered when he has a fixed sens sens & rooted sense of something better than any earth carnal object Theref. be strict in enq. how it is with you you that have heretofore been addicted to youthfull pleasure & enslaved by them has your soul ever been really [--] set at liberty from that slavery. you that heretofore have been Covetous [-] worldlings & used to Go with your back bowed down ---------- have you ever been lifted up from the Earth or dont [-] had your seeming Conv. that Effect as to make a Great change in this Respect as are you not as Covetous & Θly as ----- & as ----- as ever. you that formerly used to be enslaved by a sp. of pride & Envy & hatred of others have you been delivered from that bondage how does it prove as the tempta. Returns are you [-] turned from a wolf into a Peaceable quiet Lamb or are you as much of or have you as much of your wolfish nature still as ever

II. use may be of awakening to those that are still are are still have long been the subjects of means to raise them em fro a devotedness to Ea carnal things & still Remain go with their backs bowed down towards the Earth. It is an awfull Curse that you of G. that you are the subjects of you are a partaker of that curse that G. denounced on the serp soon after the fall of man . on thy belly shalt thou go & dust shall thou eat all the days satan the old serpent hims. in that Contemptible that G. hath & will thrust will thrust him down into and make him his own tormentor in those Restless pursuits of his that he spends so many ages in and another fulfillment of it is in the seed of the serpent or in wicked men who are laid under that curse in being Left under the power of an earthly mind & given up to Earthly pursuits that they do as it were go on their bellies on the Ground instead of walking upright as men . and it is dust that [^023-note18] they Eat this is the food they are continually hungring & pursuing after . this is what they are continually & Eagerly swallowing down. they see nothing better than this. & thus it is with them from year to year they are Confined to the Earth in their hearts & affections [-] & go no & can no otherwise than on their bellies . & the the mean dust of the Earth is all their food sensual delights & pleasures & Θ profits & honours is all their food that they have to live upon which is at best but the dust of the Earth

& in that you have Continued some of you for so long a time bent thus bowed down in your affections to the Earth you have Great Reason to fear least the sh Curse should be fulfilled upon not only in the former but also in the latter Clause of it that you shall not only go on your belly & Eat dust but that all all the days f your Life that G yo G. will bow down your back alway. & y that you never will be delivered . for Consid. what Endeavours have been used with you already in vain Consid. how many checks of Consc. & & you have already in vain & it may be many Resolutions & many attempts in vain [^023-note19] Consider those things you that have long been addicted to sensual delights . & you that have spent many years in as it were in pursuing after nothing Θly profits like a swine ----- & Grubbling in the Earth while your heart was never ascended heavenw. all this while

## 557. Ex. 20:15 557. Exod 20'15 edited

Jonathan Edwards

_______________________________________________

Exod. 20. 15. Thou shalt not steal

Cursed be the deciever that hath in his flock & a male and voweth & sacrificeth un to the L. a corrupt thing for I am a Great King saith the L. of Hosts & my name is dreadfull his flock a male i. e that has in his flock that which is good & fit to be offered to G. for it was the male of the flock principally that were appointed in the Law of moses to be flock that which is good but he [----] vows & sacrifices to the L. the torn the Lame & the sick as tis said in the foregoing verse . yet said also what a weariness is it and ye have snuffed at it saith the Lord of Hosts & ye brought that which was [L18.r] torn & the lame & the sick thus ye brought an offering should I accept this at your hands saith the L. of Hosts in the Room of sacrifices in the Jewish Chh. mercy comes in the Room of sacrifice & what is offered in a way of mercy is as much offered to G. as the sacrifices of old were. for what is done to the poor is done & he that gives to the poor Lendeth sick & Lame of the flock knew that if they had offered it to their Governour and attempted to put of it off as part of the tribute due to their Rulers Earthly rulers it would not be accepted & theref they [were] willing to save save their put if off to G. as here in the 8. v. of this Chap. ----------------------- ------------------------ so those persons that purposely put bad money into contributions they Know what they put in wont be accepted if they offered it to pay their publick taxes with it that it wont be accepted yea they know their neighbours wont accept it at their hands & theref are willing to save [L18.v] save thems. by putting it off to G.

this is also very much of the nature of the sin of Ananias & sapphira. for what they offered was by way of contribution for Charitable uses. the brethren sold what they had & brought into a common stock & put all in under the care of deacons that the poor & sapphira brought a part of what was their possession & put it into the common stock & their sin was that they put it in for more than it was . it was but half of what & would have it accepted as if it was the they put into the Charitable stock for more than it is for they put it in under the notion of it is being something of some value they intend it shall be so taken by the Chh that sees em go to the Contribution when indeed they put in nothing at all

ananias & sapphira were Charged with Lying unto G. & doing an act of fraud towards G. hims. [L19.r] for wh in what they did Acts 5. 4. while it Remained was it not thine own & after it was sold was it not in thine own Power why hath thou concieved this thing in thine Heart thou hast not lied unto men but unto bad money for Good into a Charitable us Contribution for a Charitable use they do as as much as in em lie [-] commit an act of fraud & deceit towards G. for the Chh that Recieves deacons that Recieve Recieve not in their own names but as X’ officers & the poor stands as X’ Recievers I hope these things may be sufficient to have said on this head & Enough to deter all Ever every one from ever daring to do any such thing for the future [sh notes]

If some particular Person among us had a vineyard among of the same Kind of Grapes & with those that the Chil. it would Justify persons using the same liberty when occasionally passing through it Because If some Pers one Person or rare thing & the rarity & scarcity of the fruit besides if one man was distinguished by such a possession the allowing such a liberty would h have a much greater tendency to ill Conseq. than if they were common as they were danger of many Persons to pretending occasions & making occasions to pass through th the vineyard for the sake of their fill of such Rare fruit

2. It would not be a parallel Case if Persons in General amongst us had a few vines that would be a very different thing from persons in General being such Large vineyards as they [L21.v] had in Canaan. nor would this Text in such a Case warrant Persons Eating their fill of Grapes when occasionally passing by. &

3. If all in General had vineyards as they had in the Land of canaan this text would not Justify persons going on p into their neighbours vineyard on purpose to Eat Grapes no such liberty as this is given in the Text. & if there had been such Liberty it might have been of ill Conseq. persons for the sake of saving their own Grapes might tra make a trade of Going & sending their Children into their neighbours vineyards to eat their fill from time to time.

But that Liberty given in this Text to the Chil. of Israel seems to be very parallel with the liberty taken amongst us of o to take up an apple or two & Eat as we are occasionally passing through a vi a neighbours orchard. which as the Case is circumstanced [--] as our Circumstances are we may do & Justly Presume upon it that we have the Owners Consent tis a liberty that we take & find no ill Conseq. & it was Just so with Respect to [L22.r] vineyards in in Ca Israel the Land of canaan Countreys are a rare fruit & there it would by no means be warrantable for Persons to take the same Liberty when occasionally passing by their neighbours apple tree that we reasona bly use take here when going through a neigh bours orchard. the consideration of these things will Easily show the Great abuse that is made of this Text when it is made use to Justify such a Resorting of Children and others to their neighbours fruit trees as b as is sometimes on purpose to steal the fruit take & Eat is not only not Jus at all Justified by the Law of moses but it is in it un self unreasonable sequences of it are pernicious so that a man can have no dependence of enjoying the Benefit of his own property in those things which possibly he may very much he can have no assurance but that he shall mainly be deprived of what he has & that others will have the [L22.v] principal Benefit of it & so that his End in Planting & Cultivating that whence he expected those fruits of the Earth that G has Given for the Comfort & delight of mankind will be in the main frustrated.
